# THE PERFECTED BISA YANTRA.
<note>original: "Sidha Bisa Yantra." "Sidha" means perfected or spiritually empowered; a "Yantra" is a mystical diagram used as an amulet.</note>

GENTLEMEN.—I beg to inform you that I have obtained a Bisa Yantra consisting of nine squares within an amulet. Its spiritual efficacy has been perfected over twenty-one days. It has been proven unfailingly effective for six specific purposes. It will be delivered to your door for only 1 Rupee and 5 Annas <note>approximately 1.31 Rupees in old currency</note>, which includes the costs of the *Havan* <term>Havan: a sacred fire ritual where offerings are made to deities</term> and postal charges. You will only need to perform the following rites:—

*(1) For fascination <note>meaning to place someone under your complete influence or control</note>: Bathe the amulet with cow's milk on a Sunday. After burning the incense of Guggul <term>Guggul: a fragrant resin from the Mukul myrrh tree used in Vedic incense</term> before it, tie it upon your arm. All persons in positions of authority will be under your control, and all your endeavors will be crowned with success. (2) For securing success in your work: Place it upon your head; you will be successful in lawsuits or in any other work you may undertake. (3) For causing trouble to your enemy: Expose it to fire and pronounce the name of your enemy. You will achieve victory over your enemy, and all his efforts will become fruitless. (4) For securing wealth: Worship it every day as you worship Sri Thakurji <note>a respectful title for Lord Krishna or a household deity</note>; by doing so, you will gain profit in whatever business you may be following. (5) For conceiving a son: If the wife ties it around her waist, the child in the womb will remain protected, the delivery will be at full term and attended with very little pain. (6) When obsessed by an evil spirit: Tie it around the neck; all trouble due to this spirit will disappear; the child will be protected from all diseases and all evil spirits and influences.*

NOTE—Regarding the return of a man who has gone to a foreign country, the recovery of lost money, the knowledge of future events through dreams, pilgrimage, job transfers, the acquisition of knowledge, or success in examinations: if you wish to accomplish these things in addition to the six mentioned above by means of the BISA YANTRA, you should request the BISA YANTRA which, together with the accompanying gifts, costs 2 Rupees and 7 Annas. You can accomplish the six objects mentioned previously. The remaining five objects can be accomplished as follows. They will be numbered as 7, 8, etc.

NOTE—(7) Tie the amulet to a garment worn by the man who has gone to a foreign country, expose it to the incense of GUGGUL, suspend it from a peg, and strike it with a whip seven times every morning and evening. The man will return in 3 or 11 days. (8) RECOVERY OF LOST MONEY: Place the amulet at the location from which the money was taken; the money will be recovered. (9) KNOWLEDGE IN DREAMS ON A DASHMI <note>original: "दशमी" (Dashmi), the tenth day of a lunar fortnight in the Hindu calendar</note>: On a Sunday, think of your business and place the amulet under your pillow at the time of going to bed. Knowledge of coming events will be revealed in a dream. (10) FOR PILGRIMAGE OR TRANSFER: Send the amulet to the place where you wish to go; the amulet will call you there. (11) FOR ACQUIRING KNOWLEDGE AND SUCCESS IN EXAMINATIONS: In the morning while studying, the student should repeat the following MANTRA <term>Mantra: a sacred utterance or chant believed to have spiritual power</term> seven times: *Om Hrim Shrim Phanapati* <note>original: "ओऽम् ह्रीं श्रीं फणपति" - a seed-syllable invocation likely dedicated to a serpent deity or a specific aspect of wisdom</note>. Then he should learn his lesson while keeping the amulet tied to his arm. Similarly, he should repeat the MANTRA seven times at the time of the examination. He will pass.

NOTE—Gentlemen, please note that this amulet has been prepared with great labor to achieve eleven objectives. Do kindly give it a trial and see its effect. Just as an inanimate object like a magnet draws a piece of iron, what wonder is it then if this amulet achieves success in accomplishing eleven purposes?

# Opinions of Scholars, Kashi.

**Opinion of the Shri Bharat Dharma Mahamandal, Kashi.** The review published in the illustrated monthly official journal *Nigamagam Chandrika* <note>A scholarly journal dedicated to Vedic and Agamic studies</note>, Volume 28, Number 10, Page 268, October 1923 AD, is as follows:—

->(Acknowledgment of Receipt and Summary of Review)<-

**The 81-Year Almanac**—Author: Vaidya Bhushan <note>A title meaning "Jewel of Physicians"</note> Pandit Ayodhya Prasad Ji Mishra, Astronomer/Astrologer. Price: 2.50 Rupees. Available at: Ayurvedic Dispensary, Temple of Baldau Ji, No. 86, Jhansi. In the aforementioned book, the author has fully described how to reconstruct a lost birth chart <note>term: *Nashta Janmapatra* — the process of calculating a birth chart when the birth time is unknown</note> by clarifying the Era, Shaka year, month, fortnight, lunar day, weekday, constellation, yoga, karana, desired ascendant, and the nine planets based on the constants of 12-3 or the four planets. Furthermore, he has included several useful tables such as the effects of the nine planets, Royal Yogas <note>auspicious planetary alignments</note>, Death Yogas, Longevity Yogas, and a section on auspicious timings (*Muhurta*). Examples include the compatibility chart for brides and grooms, upcoming eclipses for the next 27 years, a 4300-year calendar (yearly tables), ascendant tables, marriage timings, and the almanacs from Samvat 1600 to 1680 <note>approx. 1543 to 1623 AD</note>. To our knowledge, no such book has appeared in the Hindi language until today.

The author's labor is commendable. This book is not only useful for astrologers but is also a valuable resource for all followers of the Eternal Path <note>term: *Sanatan Dharmi* — traditional Hindus</note> who require daily lunar dates. We thank the Pandit for writing such a book.

Opinion of the **Shri Bharat Dharma Mahamandal, Kashi** <note>A prominent socio-religious organization of Orthodox Hindus founded in the late 19th century.</note>.
